# Tabler Icons (`tb`) — 5093 icons

- **Prefix:** `Tb` · **Version:** 3.44.0 · **License:** MIT · **Style:** stroke · **viewBox:** `0 0 24 24`
- **Source:** [tabler/tabler-icons](https://github.com/tabler/tabler-icons)
- Large set of crisp 24×24 outline icons drawn on a 2px stroke grid (e.g. `TbHeart`, `TbHome`, `TbBrandGithub`). Theme via `color`; tune weight with `weight`.

Import default-only per icon: `import TbExample from "@mal-icons/react/tb/TbExample"`, or named from the set barrel: `import { TbExample } from "@mal-icons/react/tb"`.
